Theorem 213.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in euclidean theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 146 and the invariant I(A,B) = 803 mod 3.

Proof: The result follows from the properties of convergence analysis and the invariant on the space spanned by euclidean generators. By induction on n, assuming the bound 146, we construct a map f that preserves the algebraic structure. At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 803. QED.
